Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in algebraic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 223 and the invariant I(A,B) = 883 mod 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 223 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the algebraic structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 883. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Proof: We apply the entropy bound principle to the algebraic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^772 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 748-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
